country_event = { #Porteñazo Resolution
	id = VEN_skeleton.0
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.0" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.0.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.0.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = VEN_skeleton.0.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_PORTENAZO option = VEN_PORTENAZO_PEACE }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VEN_Portenazo_Violence
		VEN_commie_increase_small = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = VEN_skeleton.0.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_PORTENAZO option = VEN_PORTENAZO_VIOLENCE }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.2
			}
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = socialist popularity = 0.05 }
		set_country_flag = VEN_Portenazo_Peace
		VEN_commie_decrease_small = yes
	}
}


country_event = { #1963 elections
	id = VEN_skeleton.1
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.1" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.1.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = { USA = { clr_country_flag = USA_can_intervene_in_VEN } }

	option = { #URD Victory
		name = VEN_skeleton.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_AD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_COPEI }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				NOT = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_AD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_COPEI }
				}
				USA = { has_country_flag = USA_supports_URD }
			}
		}
		if = { # Villalba (URD) Victory
			limit = { has_country_flag = VEN_Portenazo_Violence }
			set_country_flag = VEN_Villalba_1963
			country_event = {
				id = VEN_skeleton.1V
				days = 100
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MEX = {
					country_event = {
						id = MEX_VEN.34
						days = 5
					}
				}
				IBR = {
					country_event = {
						id = IBR_VEN.21
						days = 5
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = { id = USA_VEN.23 days = 102 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = { # Ojeda (URD) Victory
			limit = { has_country_flag = VEN_Portenazo_Peace }
			set_country_flag = VEN_Ojeda_1963
			#promote_character = VEN_Fabricio_Ojeda
			country_event = {
				id = VEN_skeleton.1O
				days = 100
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MEX = {
					country_event = {
						id = MEX_VEN.4
						days = 15
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = PPI_Trujillo_Victory }
					IBR = { country_event = { id = IBR_VEN.1_trujillo days = 15 } }
					USA = { country_event = { id = USA_VEN.5 days = 15 } }
				}
				else = {
					IBR = { country_event = { id = IBR_VEN.1_rebel days = 15 } }
					USA = { country_event = { id = USA_VEN.4 days = 15 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Caldera (COPEI) Victory
		name = VEN_skeleton.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_URD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_AD }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				NOT = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_URD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_AD }
				}
				USA = { has_country_flag = USA_supports_COPEI }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VEN_COPEI_1963
		country_event = {
			id = VEN_skeleton.1CA
			days = 100
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			MEX = {
				country_event = {
					id = MEX_VEN.35
					days = 5
				}
			}
			IBR = {
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_VEN.22
					days = 5
				}
			}
			USA = {
				country_event = { id = USA_VEN.24 days = 102 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #AD Victory
		name = VEN_skeleton.1.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_URD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_COPEI }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				NOT = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_URD }
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_1963_ELECTION option = VEN_1963_ELECTION_COPEI }
				}
				USA = { has_country_flag = USA_supports_AD }
			}
		}
		if = { # Barrios (AD) Victory
			limit = { has_global_flag = PPI_Rebel_Victory }
			set_country_flag = VEN_Barrios_1963
			country_event = {
				id = VEN_skeleton.1B
				days = 100
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MEX = {
					country_event = {
						id = MEX_VEN.4
						days = 15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = {
						id = USA_VEN.4
						days = 15
					}
				}
				IBR = {
					hidden_effect = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_flag = PPI_Trujillo_Victory }
							country_event = {
								id = IBR_VEN.1_trujillo
								days = 15
							}
						}
						else = {
							country_event = {
								id = IBR_VEN.1_rebel
								days = 15
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = { # Leoni (AD) Victory
			set_country_flag = VEN_Leoni_1963
			country_event = {
				id = VEN_skeleton.1L
				days = 100
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				MEX = {
					country_event = {
						id = MEX_VEN.4
						days = 15
					}
				}
				USA = {
					country_event = {
						id = USA_VEN.5
						days = 15
					}
				}
				IBR = {
					hidden_effect = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_flag = PPI_Trujillo_Victory }
							country_event = {
								id = IBR_VEN.1_trujillo
								days = 15
							}
						}
						else = {
							country_event = {
								id = IBR_VEN.1_rebel
								days = 15
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Villalba Victory
	id = VEN_skeleton.1V
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.1V" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.1V.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.1V.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = VEN_skeleton.1V.a
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = progressivism
			long_name = VEN_progressivism_1_party_long
			name = VEN_progressivism_1_party
		}
		retire_country_leader = yes
		if = { # Barrios (AD) Victory
			limit = { has_global_flag = PPI_Rebel_Victory }
			promote_character = VEN_Gonzalo_Barrios
			remove_ideas = VEN_game_of_the_three_kings
			add_ideas = VEN_game_of_the_three_kings_barrios
		}
		else = { VEN_Raul_Leoni = { promote_character = progressivism_gaitanismo_subtype } }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = liberalism
			elections_allowed = yes
			last_election = "1964.12.1"
			election_frequency = 48
		}
		set_popularities = {
			fascism = 1
			despotism = 11
			paternalism = 10
			conservatism = 9
			liberal_conservatism = 10
			liberalism = 18
			progressivism = 14
			socialist_1 = 9
			socialist = 8
			socialist_2 = 3
			communist = 7
		}
		remove_all_ministers = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.3
			}
		}
		VEN_commie_increase_small = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Villalba Coup Attempt
	id = VEN_skeleton.2
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.2" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.2.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.2.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Status Quo
		name = VEN_skeleton.2.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_SUCCESS }
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = VEN_CN_coupist_vic }
				has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_FAIL }
			}
		}
		country_event = {
			id = VEN_skeleton.10
			days = 570
		}
		set_popularities = {
			fascism = 1
			despotism = 5
			paternalism = 6
			conservatism = 11
			liberal_conservatism = 12
			liberalism = 22
			progressivism = 16
			socialist_1 = 10
			socialist = 4
			socialist_2 = 6
			communist = 7
		}
		USA = {
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 45 }
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 50 }
			Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.9
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Moreno Coup
		name = VEN_skeleton.2.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_FAIL }
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = VEN_CN_gov_vic }
				NOT = { has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_FAIL } }
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = despotism
			elections_allowed = yes
			last_election = "1964.12.1"
			election_frequency = 48
		}
		retire_country_leader = yes
		promote_character = VEN_Felix_Roman_Moreno
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = progressivism_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = progressivism_1_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = progressivism_2_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = conservatism_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = liberal_conservatism_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				expire = "1994.4.20"
				ideology = liberalism_subtype
				traits = { }
				id = -1
			}
		}
		set_popularities = {
			fascism = 1
			despotism = 13
			paternalism = 10
			conservatism = 9
			liberal_conservatism = 10
			liberalism = 16
			progressivism = 13
			socialist_1 = 9
			socialist = 5
			socialist_2 = 3
			communist = 11
		}
		remove_all_ministers = yes
		USA = {
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 45 }
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = -50 }
			Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
		}
		country_event = {
			id = VEN_skeleton.7
			days = 24
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.8
			}
		}
		USA = { country_event = { id = USA_VEN.54 days = 2 } }
	}
}

country_event = { #Barrios Coup Attempt
	id = VEN_skeleton.3
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.3" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.3.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.3.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Status Quo
		name = VEN_skeleton.3.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_SUCCESS }
					AND = {
						has_global_flag = VEN_EC_coup_victory
						NOT = { has_game_rule = { rule = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT option = VEN_COUP_ATTEMPT_FAIL } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		country_event = {
			id = VEN_skeleton.10
			days = 1896
		}

country_event = { #Post Mendoza 1968 Election
	id = VEN_skeleton.23
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.23" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.23.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.23.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Pablo_Antonio_Flores ruling_only = yes } }
		NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Juan_Dios_Moncada_Vidal ruling_only = yes } }
	}
	option = {
		name = VEN_skeleton.23.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Pedro_Tinoco ruling_only = yes } }
			retire_character = VEN_Power_Vacuum
			VEN_Jovito_Villalba = { promote_character = liberalism_national_liberal_subtype }
			VEN_Carlos_Andres_Perez = { promote_character = progressivism_left_wing_populism_subtype }

			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = paternalism
				elections_allowed = yes
				last_election = "1964.12.1"
				election_frequency = 48
			}
			set_popularities = {
				fascism = 7
				despotism = 12
				paternalism = 24
				conservatism = 11
				liberal_conservatism = 6
				liberalism = 15
				progressivism = 11
				socialist = 9
				communist = 5
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = paternalism
				long_name = VEN_IR_long
				name = VEN_IR
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = liberal_conservatism
				long_name = VEN_independent
				name = VEN_independent
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = conservatism
				long_name = VEN_FND_long
				name = VEN_FND
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = liberalism
				long_name = VEN_URD_long
				name = VEN_URD
			}
			add_ideas = VEN_the_invisible_hand
			remove_ideas = VEN_Venezuela_Reborn
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
				}
				news_event = {
					id = VEN_news.36
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } }
						AND = {
							check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR }
							OR = {
								AND = {
									country_exists = COL
									NOT = { COL = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } } }
								}
								AND = {
									country_exists = CLC
									NOT = { country_exists = COL }
								}
								AND = {
									OR = {
										country_exists = CLL
										country_exists = CLD
									}
									NOT = { country_exists = COL }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						country_exists = COL
						COL = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = USA } }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR }
						}
						IBR = { country_event = { id = IBR_VEN.53 } }
					}
					econ_join_sphere_USA = yes
					USA = { country_event = { id = USA_VEN.56 } }
				}
				else = { LATAM_Partner_Preference_selector = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } } }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_global_flag = VEN_CN_coupist_vic
								has_global_flag = VEN_CN_gov_vic
							}
						}
						GER = {
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 75 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 75 }
							Cold_War_GUI_AddVictory = yes
						}
						USA = {
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 75 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 2 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = -75 }
							Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						GER = {
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 75 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 75 }
							Cold_War_GUI_AddVictory = yes
						}
						USA = {
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 75 }
							set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 2 }

			}
		}

country_event = { #Pablo Antonio Flores Coup
	id = VEN_skeleton.100
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_skeleton.100" }
	title = VEN_skeleton.100.t
	desc = VEN_skeleton.100.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_government = fascism }
		NOT = { has_country_flag = VEN_Right_Government }
	}

	option = { #Army Coup
		name = VEN_skeleton.100.a
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = despotism
			elections_allowed = yes
			last_election = "1969.12.1"
			election_frequency = 48
		}
		set_popularities = {
			fascism = 9
			despotism = 30
			paternalism = 10
			conservatism = 6
			liberal_conservatism = 7
			liberalism = 7
			progressivism = 6
			socialist = 9
			communist = 16
		}
		retire_country_leader = yes
		promote_character = VEN_Pablo_Antonio_Flores
		econ_join_sphere_USA = yes
		remove_all_ministers = yes
		econ_leave_sphere = yes
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = despotism
			long_name = VEN_despotism_party_long
			name = VEN_despotism_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = socialist
			long_name = VNS_communist_party_long
			name = VNS_communist_party
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = communist
			long_name = VNS_communist_party_long
			name = VNS_communist_party
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				HasAmericasInterest = yes
			}
			news_event = {
				id = VEN_news.45
			}
		}
		USA = { country_event = { id = USA_VEN.60 days = 2 } }
		MEX = { country_event = { id = MEX_VEN.65 days = 2 } }
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = VEN_news.1.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_in_faction_with = GER

			}
		}

	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_news.34" }
	title = VEN_news.34.t
	desc = VEN_news.34.desc
	picture = GFX_news_event_VEN_MendozaResigns
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = VEN_news.34.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_in_faction_with = GER
				check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = VEN_news.34.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				is_in_faction_with = GER
				check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER }
				is_in_faction_with = USA
				check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = USA }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = VEN_news.34.c
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_in_faction_with = USA
				check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = USA }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Iberia Gets Informed about Alignment
	id = VEN_forpol.100
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_forpol.100" }
	title = VEN_forpol.100.t
	desc = VEN_forpol.100.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	picture = GFX_report_event_GNG_documents_on_table

	option = {
		name = VEN_forpol.100.a
		ai_chance = { base = 1 }
		add_political_power = 35
		VEN = { econ_join_sphere_IBR = yes }
		add_to_variable = { IBR_free_prod_units_variable = 1 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_gain_1_pu_tt
		every_country = {
			limit = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } }
			set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = 0.25 }
			econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Send troops to Venezuela during Palisade
	id = VEN_forpol.106
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event VEN_forpol.106" }
	title = VEN_forpol.106.t
	desc = VEN_forpol.106.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_VEN_map
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = VEN_forpol.106.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = VEN_forpol.106.tt
		if = {
			limit = { VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } } }
			IBR = { 
				set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
				TNO_increase_volunteer_cap_2 = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } } }
			GER = { 
				set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
				TNO_increase_volunteer_cap_2 = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = ITA } } }
			ITA = { 
				set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
				TNO_increase_volunteer_cap_2 = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Venezuelan Revolution - govt victory USA event
	id = USA_VEN.33
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event USA_VEN.33" }
	title = USA_VEN.33.t
	desc = USA_VEN.33.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_VEN_miraflores_palace

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #
		name = USA_VEN.33.a
		add_political_power = 75
		TNO_fopo_remove_venezuelan_revolution_tab = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = USA } }
					VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = MEX } }
				}
			}
			USA = {
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 47 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 150 }
				Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
		TNO_clear_and_recall_volunteers = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					VEN = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Marcos_Perez_Jimenez ruling_only = yes } }
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.50
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = MEX } }
					NOT = { VEN = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Pablo_Antonio_Flores ruling_only = yes } } }
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.54
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					VEN = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Pablo_Antonio_Flores ruling_only = yes } }
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.52
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.51
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Operation Lifesaver success!
	id = USA_VEN.43
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event USA_VEN.43" }
	title = USA_VEN.43.t
	desc = USA_VEN.43.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_burning_books

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #
		name = USA_VEN.43.a
		hidden_effect = {
			USA = {
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 76 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 1 }
				Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
			}
			VNS = {
				kill_country_leader = yes
				set_politics = { ruling_party = despotism }
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = VNS_Buro_Politico
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						expire = "1994.4.20"
						ideology = despotism_interim_government_subtype
						id = -1
					}
				}
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = VNS_Buro_Politico
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						expire = "1994.4.20"
						ideology = communist_subtype
						traits = { }
						id = -1
					}
				}
				#Chain
				country_event = { #Death of Stalin Venezuela Edition
					id = VEN_revolution.13
					days = 50
				}
				set_popularities = {
					despotism = 5
					socialist = 28
					communist = 33
					communist_1 = 19
					communist_2 = 15
				}
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = socialist
					name = VNS_PCV_martin
					long_name = VNS_PCV_martin_long
				}
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communist
					name = VNS_PCV_ortega
					long_name = VNS_PCV_ortega_long
				}
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communist_1
					name = VNS_PCV_bravo
					long_name = VNS_PCV_bravo_long
				}
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = communist_2
					name = VNS_PCV_silva
					long_name = VNS_PCV_silva_long
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					HasAmericasInterest = yes
				}
				news_event = {
					id = VEN_news.55
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Venezuelan Revolution proxy start for GER
	id = GER_VEN.1
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GER_VEN.1" }
	title = GER_VEN.1.t
	desc = GER_VEN.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_VEN_faln_uprising_map

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #
		name = GER_VEN.1.a
		TNO_fopo_add_venezuelan_revolution_tab = yes
		GER = {
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 47 }
			set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 0 }
			Cold_War_GUI_AddVictory = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
		set_temp_variable = { number = 1 }
		TNO_set_num_volunteers = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = GER_VEN.4 days = 16 }
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = no
					HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
				}
				news_event = {
					id = VEN_news.49
				}
			}
		}
		VEN_update_revolution_GUI = yes
	}
}

country_event = { # Venezuelan Revolution - govt victory GER event
	id = GER_VEN.8
	immediate = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event GER_VEN.8" }
	title = GER_VEN.8.t
	desc = GER_VEN.8.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_restaurant

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #
		name = GER_VEN.8.a
		add_political_power = 75
		TNO_fopo_remove_venezuelan_revolution_tab = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				VEN = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } }
			}
			GER = {
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_ID = 47 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Outcome = 4 }
				set_temp_variable = { Victory_Points = 150 }
				Cold_War_GUI_ManipulateVictory = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { target = VEN }
		TNO_clear_and_recall_volunteers = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					VEN = { has_country_leader = { character = VEN_Marcos_Perez_Jimenez ruling_only = yes } }
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.50
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						HasAxisAmericasInterest = yes
					}
					news_event = {
						id = VEN_news.51
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
